# Video Input
## Overview<a name="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002408258766"></a>

The Video Input (VI) module receives video data through MIPI Rx (including MIPI, LVDS, and HISPI interfaces), BT.1120, BT.656, BT.601, DC, and other interfaces. VI stores the received data into specified memory regions. During this process, VI can process the received raw video image data to implement video data capture.

## Important Concepts<a name="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002441657861"></a>

-   Video Input Device

    A Video Input Device supports several timing input types and is responsible for timing parsing.

-   Video Input Physical PIPE

    A Video Input PIPE is bound to the back end of a device and is responsible for further processing of data parsed by the device.

-   Video Input Virtual PIPE

    A Video Input Virtual PIPE is not bound to a device and is responsible for further processing of data sent by other modules or the user.

-   Video Physical Channel

    A Physical Channel is responsible for outputting the final processed data to DDR. Before actually outputting data to DDR, it can implement functions such as cropping.

-   PIPE Working Mode

    See"System Control"Chapter"VIandVPSS"Working ModeDescription.

-   Mask

    The mask is used to indicate the video data source of a VI device.

-   Lens Distortion Correction (LDC)

    Lens Distortion Correction: Some low-end lenses tend to produce image distortion, requiring the image to be corrected based on the degree of distortion.

-   DIS

    The DIS module compares the current image with the previous two frames using stabilization algorithms of varying degrees of freedom, calculates the shake offset vector of the current image in each axis direction, and then corrects the current image based on the shake offset vector, thereby achieving stabilization.

-   BAS

    Bayer scaling, i.e., Bayer domain scaling.

-   Early Interrupt Reporting

    Early interrupt reporting means that after a specified number of image lines have been written to DDR, VI reports an interrupt and sends the image to the back-end module for processing. This can reduce latency, but unlike Low Latency mode, there is no hardware mechanism to ensure that the back-end module will read the image without errors.

### Video Input Device<a name="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002441657925"></a>

All VI devices are independent of each other and support timing parsing.

### Video Input PIPE<a name="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002441658365"></a>

VIPIPEincludeISPRelatedProcessfunction,Mainison image dataperformpipelineProcess,OutputYUVimageFormatto Channel.PIPE Working ModeSee"System Control"Chapter"VIandVPSS"Working ModeDescription.

### Video Physical Channel<a name="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002441658401"></a>

- The Hi3403V100 VI has only one Physical Channel and supports 8 Extension Channels.
- The Hi3403V100 Physical Channel supports typical resolutions such as 3840x2160@60fps, 3840x2160@30fps, 1080p@240fps, 1080p@120fps, 1080p@60fps, 1080p@30fps, etc.

### Video Extension Channel<a name="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002441698193"></a>

An Extension Channel is an extension of the Physical Channel. Extension Channels have scaling and cropping capabilities. By binding to a Physical Channel, they take the Physical Channel output as their own input and then output the target image configured by the user.

### Slave Mode<a name="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002441697853"></a>

For Slave Mode SENSOR, the VI slave mode module must be used. The mapping between slave mode and VI Physical PIPE is fixed. The user needs to determine which slave mode module to use based on the SENSOR pin connections and [Table 1](#_Ref486405463), then select the corresponding Physical PIPE number to create a Physical PIPE; otherwise there will be no data. The detailed steps are as follows:

1. According to the hardware schematic, confirm that the SENSOR pins are connected to SENSOR\_HSx / SENSOR\_VSx.
2. According to [Table 1](#_Ref486405463), determine that this SENSOR is connected to slave mode module x.
3. The software determines, according to [Table 1](#_Ref486405463), that PIPE number x can be used when capturing data from this SENSOR.

### Mask Configuration <a name="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002441698117"></a>

The upper 12 bits of the mask correspond to the 12 pin connections on the hardware circuit (any 12 consecutive pins between D0 and D15, e.g., D4\~D15). The user must set the appropriate mask configuration based on the actual connection. The most significant bit of the mask corresponds to pin D15. For example, if a 10-bit input sensor is connected to pins D6\~D15, the mask is configured as 0xFFC00000. Similarly, for 14-bit input, the corresponding mask is configured as 0xFFFC0000.

The VI data line order is from low to high. For example, for single-component input, D0 is the low bit of data and D15 is the high bit of data.

-   1-channel/2-channel 5M or 1080p Image Input Scenario (12-bit Input)

    In 1-channel/2-channel 5M or 1080p image input scenarios, when setting VI device attributes, the mask can be configured according to [Table 1](#_Ref331669300).

**Table 1** Mask Configuration for 1-channel, 2-channel 5M or 1080p Scenarios (12-bit)

## API Reference<a name="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002408258506"></a>

The VI module implements functions such as Dev configuration and enabling, Dev and Pipe binding, Grp configuration, Pipe creation and enabling, Chn configuration and enabling, etc.

This functional module provides the following MPI APIs:

- [ss\_mpi\_vi\_set\_device\_attr](#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002441697677):SetVIDeviceAttribute.
- [ss\_mpi\_vi\_get\_device\_attr](#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002408099066):Gets VI device attributes.
- [ss\_mpi\_vi\_set\_bas\_attr](#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002441658393):Sets VI BayerScale attributes.
- [ss\_mpi\_vi\_get\_bas\_attr](#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002408098774):Gets VI BayerScale attributes.
- [ss\_mpi\_vi\_enable\_device](#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002408098518):Enables a VI device.
- [ss\_mpi\_vi\_disable\_device](#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002441658061):Disables a VI device.
- [ss\_mpi\_vi\_set\_thermo\_sns\_attr](#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002408258594):Sets the configuration attributes of a thermal imaging detector.
- [ss\_mpi\_vi\_get\_thermo\_sns\_attr](#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002441697765):Gets the configuration attributes of a thermal imaging detector.
- [ss\_mpi\_vi\_enable\_device\_send\_frame](#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002441697685):Enables the device frame sending function.
- [ss\_mpi\_vi\_disable\_device\_send\_frame](#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002441658409):Disables the device frame sending function.
- [ss\_mpi\_vi\_send\_device\_frame](#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002408258974):Configures the frame information for device frame sending.
- [ss\_mpi\_vi\_set\_device\_timing\_attr](#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002441697949):Sets device self-generated timing attributes.
- [ss\_mpi\_vi\_get\_device\_timing\_attr](#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002408258434):Gets device self-generated timing attributes.
- [ss\_mpi\_vi\_set\_device\_data\_attr](#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002408258662):Sets device self-generated data attributes.
- [ss\_mpi\_vi\_get\_device\_data\_attr](#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002408258814):Gets device self-generated data attributes.
- [ss\_mpi\_vi\_bind](#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002408098590):Binds a Dev and Pipe in a one-to-one manner.
- [ss\_mpi\_vi\_unbind](#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002441657837):Unbinds a Dev and Pipe in a one-to-one manner.
- [ss\_mpi\_vi\_get\_bind\_by\_device](#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002408259002):Gets the Pipe bound to a Dev.
- [ss\_mpi\_vi\_get\_bind\_by\_pipe](#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002441698221):Gets the Dev bound to a Pipe.
- [ss\_mpi\_vi\_set\_WDR\_fusion\_grp\_attr](#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002408258702):Sets the attributes of a WDR fusion group.
- [ss\_mpi\_vi\_get\_WDR\_fusion\_grp\_attr](#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002441657809):Gets the attributes of a WDR fusion group.
- [ss\_mpi\_vi\_create\_pipe](#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002408098762):Creates a VI PIPE.
- [ss\_mpi\_vi\_destroy\_pipe](#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002408098898):Destroys a VI PIPE.
- [ss\_mpi\_vi\_set\_pipe\_attr](#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002441697805):Sets the attributes of a VI PIPE.
- [ss\_mpi\_vi\_get\_pipe\_attr](#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002408098506):Gets the attributes of a VI PIPE.
- [ss\_mpi\_vi\_start\_pipe](#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002408259010):Enables a VI PIPE.
- [ss\_mpi\_vi\_stop\_pipe](#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002408099054):Disables a VI PIPE.
- [ss\_mpi\_vi\_set\_pipe\_pre\_crop](#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002441658117):Sets the crop function attributes at the input end of a VI Physical PIPE.
- [ss\_mpi\_vi\_get\_pipe\_pre\_crop](#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002441697633):Gets the crop function attributes at the input end of a VI Physical PIPE.
- [ss\_mpi\_vi\_set\_pipe\_post\_crop](#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002408258690):Sets the crop function attributes at the output end of a VI Physical PIPE.
- [ss\_mpi\_vi\_get\_pipe\_post\_crop](#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002408258826):Gets the crop function attributes at the output end of a VI Physical PIPE.
- [ss\_mpi\_vi\_set\_pipe\_frame\_dump\_attr](#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002408098630):Sets the frame dump attributes of a VI Physical PIPE.
- [ss\_mpi\_vi\_get\_pipe\_frame\_dump\_attr](#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002408258442):Gets the frame dump attributes of a VI Physical PIPE.
- [ss\_mpi\_vi\_get\_pipe\_frame](#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002441658209):Gets an image frame from a VI Physical PIPE.
- [ss\_mpi\_vi\_release\_pipe\_frame](#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002408258966):Releases an image frame of a VI Physical PIPE.
- [ss\_mpi\_vi\_set\_pipe\_fe\_out\_frame\_dump\_attr](#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002441657853):Sets the FE output frame dump attributes of a VI Physical PIPE.
- [ss\_mpi\_vi\_get\_pipe\_fe\_out\_frame\_dump\_attr](#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002408098342):Gets the FE output frame dump attributes of a VI Physical PIPE.
- [ss\_mpi\_vi\_get\_pipe\_fe\_out\_frame](#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002408099042):Gets the FE output image frame of a VI Physical PIPE.
- [ss\_mpi\_vi\_release\_pipe\_fe\_out\_frame](#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002408098442):Releases the FE output image frame of a VI Physical PIPE.
- [ss\_mpi\_vi\_set\_pipe\_private\_data\_dump\_attr](#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002441658077):Sets the private data dump attributes of a VI Physical PIPE.
- [ss\_mpi\_vi\_get\_pipe\_private\_data\_dump\_attr](#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002408258366):Gets the private data dump attributes of a VI Physical PIPE.
- [ss\_mpi\_vi\_get\_pipe\_private\_data](#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002408258846):Gets the private data of a VI Physical PIPE.
- [ss\_mpi\_vi\_release\_pipe\_private\_data](#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002408098670):Releases the private data of a VI Physical PIPE.
- [ss\_mpi\_vi\_set\_pipe\_bas\_frame\_dump\_attr](#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002441697701):Sets the BAS frame dump attributes of a VI PIPE.
- [ss\_mpi\_vi\_get\_pipe\_bas\_frame\_dump\_attr](#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002441697609):Gets the BAS frame dump attributes of a VI PIPE.
- [ss\_mpi\_vi\_get\_pipe\_bas\_frame](#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002441698057):Gets the BAS image frame of a VI PIPE.
- [ss\_mpi\_vi\_release\_pipe\_bas\_frame](#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002408099062):Releases the BAS image frame of a VI PIPE.
- [ss\_mpi\_vi\_set\_pipe\_frame\_source](#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002441658049):Sets the data source of a VI PIPE.
- [ss\_mpi\_vi\_get\_pipe\_frame\_source](#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002441657797):Gets the data source of a VI PIPE.
- [ss\_mpi\_vi\_set\_pipe\_param](#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002408098930):Sets VI PIPE parameters.
- [ss\_mpi\_vi\_get\_pipe\_param](#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002441697837):Gets VI PIPE parameters.
- [ss\_mpi\_vi\_enable\_pipe\_stagger\_out\_split](#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002441658385):Enables VI PIPE STAGGER mode output splitting.
- [ss\_mpi\_vi\_disable\_pipe\_stagger\_out\_split](#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002441698213):Disables VI PIPE STAGGER mode output splitting.
- [ss\_mpi\_vi\_set\_pipe\_bnr\_buf\_num](#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002408098850):Sets the number of Bayer NR buffers for a VI PIPE.
- [ss\_mpi\_vi\_get\_pipe\_bnr\_buf\_num](#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002441698025):Gets the number of Bayer NR buffers for a VI PIPE.
- [ss\_mpi\_vi\_send\_pipe\_yuv](#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002408098526):Sends YUV data through a VI PIPE.
- [ss\_mpi\_vi\_send\_pipe\_raw](#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002441697621):Sends RAW data through a VI PIPE.
- [ss\_mpi\_vi\_query\_pipe\_status](#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002441658225):Queries the status of a VI PIPE.
- [ss\_mpi\_vi\_enable\_pipe\_interrupt](#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002441698037):Enables the VI Physical PIPE interrupt.
- [ss\_mpi\_vi\_disable\_pipe\_interrupt](#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002441698177):Disables the VI Physical PIPE interrupt.
- [ss\_mpi\_vi\_set\_pipe\_vc\_number](#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002408258378):Sets the VC number for the VI Physical PIPE to interface with the front-end sensor or AD.
- [ss\_mpi\_vi\_get\_pipe\_vc\_number](#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002408258854):Gets the VC number for the VI Physical PIPE to interface with the front-end sensor or AD.
- [ss\_mpi\_vi\_set\_pipe\_low\_delay\_attr](#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002408258530):Sets VI PIPE Low Latency attributes.
- [ss\_mpi\_vi\_get\_pipe\_low\_delay\_attr](#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002408258458):Gets VI PIPE Low Latency attributes.
- [ss\_mpi\_vi\_set\_pipe\_frame\_interrupt\_attr](#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002408098466):Sets the VI PIPE interrupt reporting attributes.
- [ss\_mpi\_vi\_get\_pipe\_frame\_interrupt\_attr](#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002441698065):Gets the VI PIPE interrupt reporting attributes.
- [ss\_mpi\_vi\_set\_pipe\_fisheye\_cfg](#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002408098862):Sets the LMF parameter configuration for the fisheye lens corresponding to the VI PIPE.
- [ss\_mpi\_vi\_get\_pipe\_fisheye\_cfg](#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002441658345):Gets the LMF parameter configuration for the fisheye lens corresponding to the VI PIPE.
- [ss\_mpi\_vi\_get\_pipe\_compress\_param](#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002408098426):Gets the RAW compression parameters of the VI Physical PIPE.
- [ss\_mpi\_vi\_set\_user\_pic](#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002441698165):Sets a user picture to be used as the inserted picture when there is no video signal.
- [ss\_mpi\_vi\_enable\_user\_pic](#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002441657949):Enables user picture insertion for a VI PIPE.
- [ss\_mpi\_vi\_disable\_user\_pic](#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002441697749):Disables user picture insertion for a VI PIPE.
- [ss\_mpi\_vi\_pipe\_set\_vb\_src](#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002408098310):Sets the VB source of a VI PIPE.
- [ss\_mpi\_vi\_pipe\_get\_vb\_src](#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002408099034):Gets the VB source of a VI PIPE.
- [ss\_mpi\_vi\_pipe\_attach\_vb\_pool](#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002441658197):Binds a VI PIPE to a video buffer VB pool.
- [ss\_mpi\_vi\_pipe\_detach\_vb\_pool](#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002408099014):Unbinds a VI PIPE from a video buffer VB pool.
- [ss\_mpi\_vi\_get\_pipe\_fd](#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002408258342):Gets the file descriptor of a VI PIPE.
- [ss\_mpi\_vi\_set\_chn\_attr](#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002408099002):Sets VI channel attributes.
- [ss\_mpi\_vi\_get\_chn\_attr](#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002441697789):Gets VI channel attributes.
- [ss\_mpi\_vi\_set\_ext\_chn\_attr](#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002408098582):Sets VI Extension Channel attributes.
- [ss\_mpi\_vi\_get\_ext\_chn\_attr](#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002408098454):Gets VI Extension Channel attributes.
- [ss\_mpi\_vi\_enable\_chn](#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002408258726):Enables a VI channel.
- [ss\_mpi\_vi\_disable\_chn](#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002408258778):Disables a VI channel.
- [ss\_mpi\_vi\_set\_chn\_crop](#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002408258938):Sets the crop function attributes of a VI channel.
- [ss\_mpi\_vi\_get\_chn\_crop](#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002408258330):Gets the crop function attributes of a VI channel.
- [ss\_mpi\_vi\_set\_chn\_rotation](#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002408258578):Sets VI image rotation attributes.
- [ss\_mpi\_vi\_get\_chn\_rotation](#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002408098606):Gets VI image rotation attributes.
- [ss\_mpi\_vi\_set\_chn\_ldc\_attr](#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002408098918):Sets VI Lens Distortion Correction (LDC) attributes.
- [ss\_mpi\_vi\_get\_chn\_ldc\_attr](#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002408258318):Gets VI Lens Distortion Correction (LDC) attributes.
- [ss\_mpi\_vi\_ldc\_pos\_query\_dst\_to\_src](#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002408258738):Finds the input image coordinate point from the LDC output image coordinate point.
- [ss\_mpi\_vi\_ldc\_pos\_query\_src\_to\_dst](#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002408098876):Finds the output image coordinate point from the LDC input image coordinate point.
- [ss\_mpi\_vi\_set\_chn\_spread\_attr](#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002408258390):Sets VI channel spread attributes.
- [ss\_mpi\_vi\_get\_chn\_spread\_attr](#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002408098402):Gets VI channel spread attributes.
- [ss\_mpi\_vi\_set\_chn\_fisheye](#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002441657997):Sets the fisheye attributes corresponding to a VI channel.
- [ss\_mpi\_vi\_get\_chn\_fisheye](#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002441697773):Gets the fisheye attributes corresponding to a VI channel.
- [ss\_mpi\_vi\_fisheye\_pos\_query\_dst\_to\_src](#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002408258834):Finds the source image coordinate point from the fisheye-corrected output image coordinate point.
- [ss\_mpi\_vi\_get\_chn\_rgn\_luma](#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002441697453):Gets the total luminance of a specified image region.
- [ss\_mpi\_vi\_set\_chn\_dis\_cfg](#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002408258606):Sets VI channel DIS configuration information.
- [ss\_mpi\_vi\_get\_chn\_dis\_cfg](#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002408098686):Gets VI channel DIS configuration information.
- [ss\_mpi\_vi\_set\_chn\_dis\_attr](#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002408098286):Sets VI channel DIS attributes.
- [ss\_mpi\_vi\_get\_chn\_dis\_attr](#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002441657637):Gets VI channel DIS attributes.
- [ss\_mpi\_vi\_set\_chn\_dis\_param](#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002441697485):Sets VI channel DIS optional parameters.
- [ss\_mpi\_vi\_get\_chn\_dis\_param](#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002408258474):Gets the optional parameters of VI channel DIS.
- [ss\_mpi\_vi\_set\_chn\_dis\_WDR\_attr](#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002468735433):Sets VI channel DIS WDR attributes.
- [ss\_mpi\_vi\_get\_chn\_dis\_WDR\_attr](#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002435176804):Gets VI channel DIS WDR attributes.
- [ss\_mpi\_vi\_set\_chn\_fov\_correction\_attr](#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002408098698):Sets VI channel FOV correction attributes.
- [ss\_mpi\_vi\_get\_chn\_fov\_correction\_attr](#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002441697585):Gets VI channel FOV correction attributes.
- [ss\_mpi\_vi\_get\_chn\_frame](#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002441698001):Gets a captured image from a VI channel.
- [ss\_mpi\_vi\_release\_chn\_frame](#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002408098798):Releases one frame of image obtained from a VI channel.
- [ss\_mpi\_vi\_set\_chn\_low\_delay\_attr](#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002408098478):Sets VI channel Low Latency attributes.
- [ss\_mpi\_vi\_get\_chn\_low\_delay\_attr](#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002408098418):Gets VI channel Low Latency attributes.
- [ss\_mpi\_vi\_set\_chn\_align](#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002441658013):Sets the row stride alignment for YUV data output by a VI channel.
- [ss\_mpi\_vi\_get\_chn\_align](#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002408098562):Gets the row stride alignment for YUV data output by a VI channel.
- [ss\_mpi\_vi\_chn\_set\_vb\_src](#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002408258930):Sets the VB source used by a VI channel.
- [ss\_mpi\_vi\_chn\_get\_vb\_src](#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002441697573):Gets the VB source used by a VI channel.
- [ss\_mpi\_vi\_chn\_attach\_vb\_pool](#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002441658173):Binds a VI channel to a video buffer VB pool.
- [ss\_mpi\_vi\_chn\_detach\_vb\_pool](#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002441658129):Unbinds a VI channel from a video buffer VB pool.
- [ss\_mpi\_vi\_query\_chn\_status](#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002441697645):Queries VI channel status.
- [ss\_mpi\_vi\_get\_chn\_fd](#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002441697533):Gets the file descriptor of a VI channel.
- [ss\_mpi\_vi\_set\_stitch\_grp\_attr](#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002441658233):Sets the stitch group attributes of VI.
- [ss\_mpi\_vi\_get\_stitch\_grp\_attr](#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002408258494):Gets the stitch group attributes of VI.
- [ss\_mpi\_vi\_set\_mod\_param](#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002441658357):Sets VI module parameters.
- [ss\_mpi\_vi\_get\_mod\_param](#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002441657753):Gets VI module parameters.
- [ss\_mpi\_vi\_close\_fd](#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002441697973):Closes a VI file descriptor.
- [ss\_mpi\_vi\_chn\_set\_zoom\_in\_window](#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002527031287):Sets VI channel crop and zoom-in attributes.
- [ss\_mpi\_vi\_chn\_get\_zoom\_in\_window](#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002494991556):Gets VI channel crop and zoom-in attributes.































































































































### ss\_mpi\_vi\_set\_device\_attr<a name="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002441697677"></a>

[Description]

Sets VI device attributes. The basic device attributes have default chip configurations that meet the requirements of most sensor connections.

**Syntax**

```
td_s32 ss_mpi_vi_set_device_attr(ot_vi_device vi_device, const ot_vi_device_attr *device_attr);
```

**Parameters**

<a name="table686mcpsimp"></a>
<table><thead align="left"><tr id="row692mcpsimp"><th class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="18%" id="mcps1.1.4.1.1"><p id="p694mcpsimp"><a name="p694mcpsimp"></a><a name="p694mcpsimp"></a>Parameter Name</p>
</th>

**Note**

-   not supportedBT.1120interlacedInput。
-   in/at before calling requireensure VIdevice placein/at Disables status 。ifVIdevicealready  placein/at enablestatus ，can use [ss\_mpi\_vi\_disable\_dev](#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002441658061) toDisables device。
-   parameterdev\_attrmainly used to configurespecified VIdevice's video interfaceMode，used for with/and outside 围camera、sensoror codecfor/to  connect/receive，supports's interfaceModeincluding MIPI Rx（MIPI/LVDS/HISPI）、SLVS-EC。user mustconfigurebelow 几类information ，specific attribute意义refer to “[Data Types](#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002408258714)”some/partial 's **Note:** 
    -   Interface mode information: interface modes such as MIPI Rx (MIPI/LVDS/HISPI)
    -   Operating mode: 1-channel, 2-channel, 4-channel composite mode
    -   Data layout information: multi-channel data arrangement in composite mode
    -   Data information: progressive input, YUV data input order
    -   - Synchronization timing information: Vertical and horizontal sync signal attributes

None.

**Solution Differences**

None.

**Requirements**

- Header: ot\_common\_vi.h, ss\_mpi\_vi.h
- Library: libss\_mpi.a

**Note**

- The Physical PIPE bound to this VI device must be destroyed before disabling the VI device.
- Can be disabled repeatedly without returning failure.
- Low-power processing is supported. After disabling the VI device, the device will be completely shut down, requiring reconfiguration of attributes before the VI device can be enabled again.

**Example**

None.

[ss\_mpi\_vi\_enable\_device](#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002408098518)

</td>
</tr>
</tbody>
</table>

**Solution Differences**

None.

**Requirements**

- Header: ot\_common\_vi.h, ss\_mpi\_vi.h
- Library: libss\_mpi.a

**Note**

- The device must be enabled before configuring frame sending through this interface, otherwise an error will be returned.
- Must be used in pairs with [ss\_mpi\_vi\_disable\_device\_send\_frame](#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002441658409), otherwise VB leakage will occur.
- When the device self-generated timing is used to feed RAW in WDR mode, pipe frame rate control must not be used to control WDR long/short frame rates, otherwise WDR frame drops due to long/short frame mismatch may occur.

**Example**

None.

**Related topics**

[ss\_mpi\_vi\_disable\_device\_send\_frame](#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002441658409)

</td>
</tr>
</tbody>
</table>

**Solution Differences**

None

**Requirements**

Header: ot\_common\_vi.h, ss\_mpi\_vi.h

**Note**

- Before using this interface, DEV attributes must be configured and the device must be enabled, otherwise failure is returned.
- When using the self-generated timing function to feed RAW, the DEV width and height must be configured consistently with the width and height of the RAW file.
- After enabling self-generated timing, if no RAW is fed, no image will be displayed.
- After enabling self-generated timing, the VI output frame rate after feeding RAW from DEV is determined by the effective frame rate generated by the self-generated timing configuration.
- When DEV is configured for X2 rate, enabling self-generated timing will cause timing anomalies.

**Example**

None.

**Related topics**

- [ss\_mpi\_vi\_set\_device\_attr](#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002441697677)
- [ss\_mpi\_vi\_enable\_device](#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002408098518)

<td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="75%" headers="mcps1.1.3.1.2 "><p id="p2213mcpsimp"><a name="p2213mcpsimp"></a><a name="p2213mcpsimp"></a>On failure, the value is <span xml:lang="fr-FR" id="ph2215mcpsimp"><a name="ph2215mcpsimp"></a><a name="ph2215mcpsimp"></a>Error Code</span></a>.</p>
</td>
</tr>
</tbody>
</table>

**Solution Differences**

None.

**Requirements**

Header: ot\_common\_vi.h, ss\_mpi\_vi.h

**Note**

-   Before using this interface, first call the DEV and PIPE bind interface, and all pipe IDs in grp\_attr must be bound to the same Dev.
-   This interface must be called before creating a pipe.
-   This interface supports updating the pipe\_id in grp\_attr (e.g., switching between WDR mode and linear mode), but binding the same pipe\_id to two grp\_attrs is not supported.
-   The pipe\_ids in a WDR fusion group cannot be duplicated.
-   In WDR mode, the primary pipe defaults to the pipe with the smallest pipe ID. For example, if a WDR fusion group has pipe IDs 2, 1, 3, the primary pipe is pipe 1. If pipe\_reverse is set to TRUE, the primary pipe is the one with the largest pipe\_ide, i.e., pipe3。
-   pipe\_reverse=TRUE only supports row-mode WDR and VI Offline mode.
-   WDR is not supported when the video mode is Advanced Mode.

**Example**

None.

**Related topics**

None.

**Requirements**

- Header: ot\_common\_vi.h, ss\_mpi\_vi.h
- Library: libss\_mpi.a

**Note**

-   Physical PIPE attributes such as size, pixel\_format, bit\_width must be consistent with the timing settings of the front-end entering VI; otherwise errors will occur.
-   Repeated creation is not supported.
-   When VI is in Offline mode and the input image exceeds 4096, compression is not supported.
-   In WDR mode, multiple physical PIPEs must be created and bound to the same WDR-enabled device. When switching, all physical PIPEs bound to that device must be destroyed and recreated. Do not use previously timeuse past/too  and/whilehas not been Destroys a 's physical PIPE，otherwisemay  cause/create becomeerror 。

None.

**Solution Differences**

None.

**Requirements**

- Header: ot\_common\_vi.h, ss\_mpi\_vi.h
- Library: libss\_mpi.a

**Note**

-   The PIPE must have been created 
-   PIPE attributes must be valid. Some static attributes cannot be set dynamically; for details, see [ot\_vi\_pipe\_attr](#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002441657721).
-   When configured as OT\_VI\_PIPE\_BYPASS\_BE, VI channel functions (such as mirror/flip) do not take effect.
-   When OT\_COMPRESS\_MODE\_FRAME is configured, PIPE Low Latency, early interrupt reporting attributes, and other functions cannot be used.

**Example**

None.

**Related topics**

None

-   The PIPE must be created first; otherwise failure is returned.
-   This interface must be configured before starting the PIPE; it cannot be called after the PIPE is started.
-   crop\_info.rect.x + width must be greater than or equal to pipe\_attr.size.width and less than or equal to the BAS output width; the same applies to height.
-   Crop attribute x/y must be 2-pixel aligned; width/height must be 4-pixel aligned.
-   After configuring pre\_crop, configure the ISP PUB attributes to be less than or equal to the Pre Crop width/height.
-   Virtual PIPE is not supported.

**Example**

None.

**Related topics**

None.

**Solution Differences**

None.

**Requirements**

Header: ot\_common\_vi.h, ss\_mpi\_vi.h

**Note**

-   The PIPE must have been created  andstart。
-    sendYUVfirst must via [ss\_mpi\_vi\_set\_pipe\_frame\_source](#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002441658049) toSets the PIPE's data source is/for OT\_VI\_PIPE\_FRAME\_SOURCE\_USER。
-   YUVdata 's width/heightmustwith/and PIPEattribute's size's width/heightmaintain/keep consistent 。
-   送下来的YUV帧信息必须为真实有效的VB。
-   YUVdata pixel formatmustwith/and PIPEattribute's pixel formatmaintain/keep consistent 
-   YUVdata fieldmustis/for OT\_VIDEO\_FIELD\_FRAME。
-   YUVdata video\_formatmustis/for OT\_VIDEO\_FORMAT\_LINEAR。
-   YUVdata compress\_modemustis/for  non- compressOT\_COMPRESS\_MODE\_NONE。
-   YUVdata dynamic range dynamic\_rangemustis/for OT\_DYNAMIC\_RANGE\_SDR8。YUVdata  color domainmustis/for valid  value\[OT\_COLOR\_GAMUT\_BT601，OT\_COLOR\_GAMUT\_BUTT\)。
-   WDRModenot supportedthis  interface must have been called first.
-   from BE sendYUV when,  toself-SENSOR's data will not  sendto BEprocessing ，BEonlywill processing user passed down fromframedata.
-   VIOnlineVPSSOnline Mode、VIOnlineVPSSOffline Modenot supportedthis  interface must have been called first.

**Example**

None.

**Related topics**

None.

**Solution Differences**

None.

**Requirements**

Header: ot\_common\_vi.h, ss\_mpi\_vi.h

**Note**

-   The PIPE must have been created  andstart。
-   vi\_pipe必须为wdr融合的主pipe号。
-    sendRAWfirst must via [ss\_mpi\_vi\_set\_pipe\_frame\_source](#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002441658049) toSets the PIPE's data source is/for OT\_VI\_PIPE\_FRAME\_SOURCE\_USER。
-   送一帧处理一帧，不会循环处理。
-   WDR的所有PIPE为Offline Mode，每个PIPE的RAW存放的顺序必须是短中长帧的顺序，目的是保证长短曝光帧的顺序正确，每个PIPE的RAW的属性\(宽高，像素格式，压缩等等\)要保持一致。
-   PIPEattribute's IspBypassmustis/for TD\_FALSE。
-   送下来的RAW帧信息必须为真实有效的VB。
-   RAWdata pixel formatmustwith/and PIPEattribute's pixel formatmaintain/keep consistent 。
-   RAWdata fieldmustis/for OT\_VIDEO\_FIELD\_FRAME。
-   RAWdata video\_formatmustis/for OT\_VIDEO\_FORMAT\_LINEAR。
-   RAWdata compress\_modemustis/for OT\_COMPRESS\_MODE\_NONE、OT\_COMPRESS\_MODE\_LINE、OT\_COMPRESS\_MODE\_FRAME。
-   RAWdata dynamic range dynamic\_rangemustis/for OT\_DYNAMIC\_RANGE\_SDR8。RAWdata  color domainmustis/for valid  value\[OT\_COLOR\_GAMUT\_BT601，OT\_COLOR\_GAMUT\_BUTT\)。
-   VIOnlineVPSSOnline Mode、VIOnlineVPSSOffline Modenot supportedthis  interface must have been called first.

**Example**

None.

**Related topics**

None.

**Solution Differences**

None.

**Requirements**

- Header: ot\_common\_vi.h, ss\_mpi\_vi.h
- Library: libss\_mpi.a

**Note**

-   Only VI offline mode is supported.
-   WDRModebelow/under from pipenot supportedSets the user picture。
-   此接口只支持YUV图像Mode，纯色Mode需要用户分配VB调用VGS打COVER实现。
-   设置VI用户图片，用于将VI通道Output的视频帧图像修改为用户配置的指定YUV图像数据，而不是codec的视频数据；此接口需要与[ss\_mpi\_vi\_enable\_user\_pic](#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002441657949)、[ss\_mpi\_vi\_disable\_user\_pic](#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002441697749)配合使用。
-   当前端None时序时，用户图片的帧率依赖于PIPE属性里面的目标帧率配置，如果PIPE属性的目标帧率设置成15帧，这个PIPE的用户图片的帧率则为15帧，通道帧率则根据通道属性的帧率来确定。如果PIPE属性的目标帧率设置成-1，用户图片的帧率则为30帧。当前端有时序时，用户图片的帧率由前端时序帧率和目标帧率配置共同决定。
-   设置完用户图片后，即可调用ss\_mpi\_vi\_enable\_user\_pic接口对指定VI PIPE启用插入用户图片，此时VI PIPE的通道Output的数据即为所配置的图片YUV数据；一般用于视频信号丢失时，VI PIPE的通道OutputNoVideo图片。

    configure's user picture's width/heightmustwith/and VI PIPEsize  mutualconsistent 。

-   user picture's video frameinformation structure in/within ，Must set figureslice 's width  degree、height  degree、line  inter-隔、YUVformatand Y sub- amount/quantityand C sub- amount/quantitydata 's physical address；can from MPP publictogetherbuffer  poolin/within Gets the one block  mutual applysize 's video bufferblock ，from bufferblock information in/within resulting in to  store/exist enlargeYUVdata 's physical address， then/naturallyafter will physical address映射to user space , i.e.,  canfor/to this block memory perform YUVdata 's fill/padding operation ；**fill/padding data  whenmust遵循first  store/existY sub- amount/quantitydata ，again/then  store/existUV sub- amount/quantity inter-插data 's storage sequence/order （small  endbyte  sequencefirst Vafter U）。**
-   VI模块在启用插入用户图片时，会直接使用设置的用户图片帧信息中的物理地址，因此设置完用户图片后，不应该释放或销毁其视频缓存块，除非确认不再使用。可以通过再次调用此接口设置另外一块 VideoBuffer以修改图片信息。
-   此功能与灌图功能互斥。若开启用户图片的同时，设置frame\_source为user进行PIPE灌图，则实际Output的为pipe灌图数据。
-   图像Mode送下来的YUV帧信息必须为真实有效的VB。
-   imageModeYUVdata pixel formatsupportsOT\_PIXEL\_FORMAT\_YVU\_SEMIPLANAR\_420、OT\_PIXEL\_FORMAT\_YVU\_SEMIPLANAR\_422、OT\_PIXEL\_FORMAT\_YUV\_400。
-   imageModeYUVdata fieldmustis/for OT\_VIDEO\_FIELD\_FRAME。
-   imageModeYUVdata video\_formatmustis/for OT\_VIDEO\_FORMAT\_LINEAR。
-   imageModeYUVdata compress\_modemustis/for  non- compressOT\_COMPRESS\_MODE\_NONE。
-   imageModeYUVdata dynamic range dynamic\_rangemustis/for OT\_DYNAMIC\_RANGE\_SDR8。
-   imageModeYUVdata  color domainmustis/for valid  value\[OT\_COLOR\_GAMUT\_BT601，OT\_COLOR\_GAMUT\_BUTT\)。

**Related topics**

- [ss\_mpi\_vi\_enable\_user\_pic](#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002441657949)
- [ss\_mpi\_vi\_disable\_user\_pic](#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002441697749)

-   Extension Channel's Inputbind sourcemustis/for Physical Channel。
-   Extension Channel以绑定的方式连接Physical Channel，每个Extension Channel最多绑定一个Physical Channel。
-   This interface is not supported in VI online VPSS online mode or VI offline VPSS online mode.
-   Extension Channelenablecompact segmentcompression after, not supported doCoverEXwith/and OverlayEX's processing , i.e., make/enable Sets the CoverEX/OverlayEXalso will not  generateeffect.
-   Extension Channel's imagesource attributeSets the is/for OT\_EXT\_CHN\_SRC\_TYPE\_HEAD（refer to ”System Control”chapter ） when, is bind's Physical Channelnot supported doCoverEXwith/and OverlayEXprocessing , i.e., make/enable Sets the CoverEX/OverlayEXalso will not  generateeffect.

None.

None.

**Requirements**

- Header: ot\_common\_vi.h, ss\_mpi\_vi.h, ot\_common\_video.h
- Library: libss\_mpi.a

**Note**

-   The PIPE must be created first; otherwise failure is returned.
-   This interfaceonlysupportsOT\_LDC\_V1version 's LDCcorrection  positive/correct。
-   LDC must be enabled before calling this interface; otherwise failure is returned.
-   After enabling LDC, if parameters need to be changed, follow the sequence: change parameters, retrieve the corrected image, then start coordinate query. Retrieving a corrected image before LDC parameter changes may lead to incorrect coordinate lookup results.
-   This interface is not supported in VI online VPSS online mode or VI offline VPSS online mode.

**Example**

None.

**Related topics**

[ss\_mpi\_vi\_set\_chn\_ldc\_attr](#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002408098918)

**Note**

-   The PIPE must have been created ，otherwisewill returns Failure
-   callthis interfacebefore Physical Channelattributealready configure。
-   enablefisheyecorrection  positive/correct after, not supporteddynamic change channelCropattribute、channelrotation attributeand channelwidth/height attribute.
-   This interface is not supported in VI online VPSS online mode or VI offline VPSS online mode.
-   fisheye correction/calibrate positive/correctthree  type correction/calibrate positive/correctMode: 
    -   360全scene Modesupports地装and 顶装；
    -   180全scene Modesupports壁装；
    -   normalModesupports地装，顶装，壁装。

-   fisheyecorrection  positive/correcthas/have minimum 's InputOutputwidth/heightlimit : OT\_FISHEYE\_MIN\_IN\_IMAGE\_HEIGHT, etc.refer to “geometric distortion correction  positive/correctsub-system ”。

**Example**

None.

**Note**

-   The PIPE must be created first; otherwise failure is returned.
-   mustfisheye correction/calibrate positive/correctstartafter again/then call，otherwisewill returns Failure。
-   启动鱼眼矫正后，如果需要变动参数，要遵循参数变动、获取校正图像和启动Coordinates点查询的先后原则。如果在鱼眼参数变动之前就获取了矫正图像，可能导致查找到错误的Coordinates点。
-   When multiple regions are used, the coordinates for each region are calculated independently; they cannot be calculated based on the stitched image.
-   This interface is not supported in VI online VPSS online mode or VI offline VPSS online mode.

**Example**

None.

**Related topics**

None.

### ss\_mpi\_vi\_get\_chn\_rgn\_luma<a name="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002441697453"></a>

[Description]

Gets the luminance sum of a specified image region. This interface is mainly used to perform luminance sum statistics on cover regions. Based on the measured luminance, inverted color processing is applied to make the cover region more visually distinct.

**Syntax**

```
td_s32 ss_mpi_vi_get_chn_rgn_luma(ot_vi_pipe vi_pipe, ot_vi_chn vi_chn, td_u32 num, const ot_rect *rgn, td_u64 *luma_data, td_s32 milli_sec);
```

None.

- Library: libss\_mpi.a

**Note**

-   The PIPE must be created first; otherwise failure is returned.
-   此接口针对的是VI通道的Output图像。获取亮度统计信息的时机是这些图像被用户获取或被发送到后端模块之前。
-   The statistics region start coordinates depend on the CROP setting: if CROP is not enabled, coordinates are relative to the top-left corner of the raw image; if CROP is enabled, coordinates are relative to the top-left corner of the cropped image. The statistics region must not exceed the raw image or cropped image boundary.
-   This interface is not supported in VI online VPSS online mode or VI offline VPSS online mode.

**Example**

None.

**Related topics**

None.

**Solution Differences**

None.

**Requirements**

- Header: ot\_common\_vi.h, ot\_common\_dis.h, ss\_mpi\_vi.h
- Library: libss\_mpi.a

**Note**

-   The PIPE must be created first; otherwise failure is returned.
-   This interface is not supported in VI online VPSS online mode or VI offline VPSS online mode.
-   parameteronlyin/at GYROModebelow/under has/have effect，GMEModebelow/under Noneeffect.parameteronlyin/at WDR\_modeis/for 2to1 WDR、3to1 WDRModebelow/under has/have effect。
-   在fswdr\_mode为OT\_ISP\_FSWDR\_LONG\_FRAME\_MODEMode时固定匹配长帧，参数设置None效；fswdr\_mode为OT\_ISP\_FSWDR\_AUTO\_LONG\_FRAME\_MODEMode时，若切换到了长帧Mode，也会固定匹配长帧，此时参数设置None效。
-   该接口为可选调用，不调用使用默认值，开启WDR和GYRO\_DIS后默认 match\_frame 为长帧。
-   使用dis\_wdr\_attr功能时需要调用ss\_mpi\_vb\_set\_supplement\_cfg（请参考“System Control”章节）接口添加VB的图像运动附加信息，对应的MASK为OT\_VB\_SUPPLEMENT\_MOTION\_DATA\_MASK。
-   match\_frame parametermeaning : WDRModeis/for 2TO1 when, 0is/for match short frame，1is/for match long frame；WDRModeis/for 3TO1 when, 0is/for short frame，1is/for in/within frame，2is/for long frame。
-   WDRMode's gyroscope stabilization mainly 针for/to match frameimage作防抖correction  positive/correct， non-match framesome/partial 's image防抖effect differencein/at 预期's gyroscope stabilization effect。

**Example**

None.

**Related topics**

[ss\_mpi\_vi\_get\_chn\_dis\_WDR\_attr](#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002435176804)

-   alignis/for Non-zero whenuse specified 's stridealignment。
-   when alignless than  chip/coreslice requirements 's stridealignment when, system allocate bufferand calculate stridealso/still is so as to  chip/coreslice requirements stridealignmentis/for  accurate/standard。
-   when alignnot equal to  chip/coreslice requirements 's stridealignmentmultiple  when, system allocate bufferand calculate strideto on/above alignmentto  chip/coreslice requirements stridealignment's multiple 。

     example: alignSets the is/for 48， chip/coreslice requirements 32alignment when, system allocate bufferand calculate stride whenuse 64alignment。

-   拍照后处理算法Input的YUV数据stride需要128Bytes对齐，因此需要调用本接口配置VIOutput的YUV数据stride对齐。
-   This interface is not supported in VI online VPSS online mode or VI offline VPSS online mode.

None.

**Note**

-   pipemustalready Creates a 。
-   if crop\_modeis/for OT\_COORD\_RATIO（ mutualfor/to CoordinatesMode）: 

    使用crop\_rect的成员时应进行转换，计算公式为： 

    x = 起始点Coordinates\*原始图像宽度/1000，合法取值范围：\[0, 999\]。计算完成后将进行取整和对齐操作。公式同样适用于纵Coordinates计算，x为转换后的Coordinates。

    width=区域宽度\*实际图像宽度/1000，区域宽度取值范围：\[1, 1000\]。计算完成后将进行取整和对齐操作。公式同样适用于区域高度计算，width为转换后的宽度。

-   in/at  mutualfor/to Modeperform crop when, crop region'sCoordinatesthe value range is \[0, 999\]，crop region'swidth/heightthe value range is \[1, 1000\]。
-   如果裁剪放大区域超出图像范围，则自动裁剪至图像的有效范围区域。
-   crop region'sstarting pointCoordinatesand width/height needsatisfy 2pixel alignmentrequirements 。

-   None论是相对还是绝对裁剪放大，实际裁剪放大区域的宽高不能小于Input图像宽高的1/8，起始点必须位于图像范围内，不允许使用负Coordinates。
-   zoom\_in\_window不支持与gyro组合。
-   when channel's width/heightchange  when, must  removere-Sets the zoomattribute，otherwisemay causes effect different/abnormal often/usual。

None.

## Data Types<a name="ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002408258714"></a>

Video input related data type definitions are as follows:

- [OT\_VI\_MAX\_SNS\_CFG\_NUM](#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002441657781): Maximum number of timing configuration data entries.
- [OT\_VI\_MAX\_SD\_MUX\_NUM](#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002408258982): Maximum number of SD pin multiplexing entries.
- [OT\_VI\_MAX\_DEV\_NUM](#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002441698073):Defines the maximum number of VI devices.
- [OT\_VI\_MAX\_PHYS\_PIPE\_NUM](#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002441698249):Defines the maximum number of VI Physical PIPEs.
- [OT\_VI\_MAX\_VIRT\_PIPE\_NUM](#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002441698185):Defines the maximum number of VI Virtual PIPEs.
- [OT\_VI\_MAX\_PIPE\_NUM](#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002408258246):Defines the maximum number of VI PIPEs.
- [OT\_VI\_MAX\_STITCH\_GRP\_NUM](#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002408098750):Defines the maximum number of VI stitch groups.
- [OT\_VI\_MAX\_WDR\_FUSION\_GRP\_NUM](#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002441658157):Defines the maximum number of VI WDR fusion groups.
- [OT\_VI\_MAX\_WDR\_FRAME\_NUM](#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002441657761): Defines the maximum number of WDR fusion composite frames for VI.
- [OT\_VI\_MAX\_PHYS\_CHN\_NUM](#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002408099078):Defines the maximum number of VI Physical Channels.
- [OT\_VI\_MAX\_EXT\_CHN\_NUM](#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002408258522):Defines the maximum number of VI Extension Channels.
- [OT\_VI\_EXT\_CHN\_START](#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002441698257):Defines the starting channel number for VI Extension Channels.
- [OT\_VI\_MAX\_EXTCHN\_BIND\_PER\_CHN](#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002408098646):Defines the maximum number of Extension Channels that can be bound to a single VI channel.
- [OT\_VI\_MAX\_CHN\_NUM](#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002441657673):Defines the total number of VI Physical Channels and Extension Channels.
- [OT\_VI\_DEV\_MIN\_WIDTH](#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002408258678):Minimum capture image width for a VI device.
- [OT\_VI\_DEV\_MIN\_HEIGHT](#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002441658141):Minimum capture image height for a VI device.

- ThisPartsyncInformationDetailedConfigure canreference"MPP MediabodyProcessSoftwareV5.0 FAQ"2.2smallsave"VI YUVTimingConfigure ".
- BT.601Modein,VerticalSync SignalonlyneedConfigure vsyncandvsync\_neg, i.e., can,TheyRespectivelyindicatesPinOT\_VI\_P\_VSYNC\_FIELDVerticalSync SignalTypeandVerticalSync SignalPolarity,two者Determines采using何kindVertical SyncTiming.
 - vsyncconfigured as OT\_VI\_VSYNC\_FIELD,BT.601ModeThe table belowshownFieldnumber.at this point,ifvsync\_negconfigured as OT\_VI\_VSYNC\_NEG\_HIGH,thenhighLevelindicatesEvenField,lowLevelindicatesOddField;ifvsync\_negconfigured as OT\_VI\_VSYNC\_NEG\_LOW,thenlowLevelindicatesEvenField,highLevelindicatesOddField.
 - vsyncconfigured as OT\_VI\_VSYNC\_PULSE,indicatesVertical Syncpulse,, i.e., apulseindicatesnewoneFieldoroneframe.at this point,ifvsync\_negconfigured as OT\_VI\_VSYNC\_NEG\_HIGH,thenpositivepulseindicatesVertical Syncpulse;ifvsync\_negconfigured as OT\_VI\_VSYNC\_NEG\_LOW,thennegativepulseindicatesVertical Syncpulse.

- DC/BT.656/BT.1120Modein,VerticalSync SignalrequireConfigure vsync, vsync\_validand vsync\_valid\_negthree,TheyRespectivelyindicatesPinOT\_VI\_P\_VSYNC\_FIELDVerticalSync SignalType, VerticalSync SignalmarkidentificationandVerticalValid SignalPolarity.vsync\_valid\_negonlyatvsync\_valid configure/pair as OT\_VI\_VSYNC\_VALID\_SIGwhenValid.
 - vsyncconfigured as OT\_VI\_VSYNC\_PULSE,indicatesVerticalTimingpulseMethod.ifatVerticalBlanking AreawithinhsyncSignalstillValid,thenvsync\_validneedconfigured as OT\_VI\_VSYNC\_VALID\_SIGSelectsVerticalValidmarkidentification,simultaneouslyvsync\_valid\_negrequireconfigured as OT\_VI\_VSYNC\_VALID\_NEG\_HIGH(orOT\_VI\_VSYNC\_VALID\_NEG\_LOW),toindicateshigh(low)LevelValid,otherSituationnotneedclose注ThisAttribute.
 - ifInputTiming as VerticalTimingRowValidMethod,onlyneedwillvsyncconfigured as OT\_VI\_VSYNC\_FIELD, i.e., can.
 - StandardBT.656, BT.1120syncInformationConfigure canreference"MPP MediabodyProcessSoftwareV5.0 FAQ"2.2smallsave"VI YUVTimingConfigure ".

- hsyncand hsync\_negRespectivelyindicatesPinVI\_P\_HSYNC\_VDHorizontalSync SignalTypeandHorizontalSync SignalPolarity,two者Determines采using何kindHorizontal SyncTiming.
 - hsyncconfigured as OT\_VI\_HSYNC\_VALID\_SIG,indicatesDataValid Signal.at this point,ifhsync\_negconfigured as OT\_VI\_HSYNC\_NEG\_HIGH,thenhighLevelindicatesDataValid;ifhsync\_negconfigured as OT\_VI\_HSYNC\_NEG\_LOW,thenlowLevelindicatesDataValid.
 - hsyncconfigured as OT\_VI\_HSYNC\_PULSE,indicatesHorizontal SyncpulseSignal,, i.e., apulseindicatesnewoneRowenable始.at this point,ifhsync\_negconfigured as OT\_VI\_HSYNC\_NEG\_HIGH,thenpositivepulseindicatesHorizontal Syncpulse;ifhsync\_negconfigured as OT\_VI\_HSYNC\_NEG\_LOW,thennegativepulseindicatesHorizontal Syncpulse.

forBlanking Information,HorizontalorVerticalValidDataMethodnotrequire configure/pairBlanking Area.

- Parameterbit\_widthindicatesInputDataType as YUVwhenDatabitwide,cannotdynamicallySet,Hi3403V100Not supported10bitYUVDataInput.
